Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a nonlinear algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 754. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 133/5.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 754 + O(1/n^133). Summing over all modes and applying the nonlinear convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.

Theorem 220.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let ρ be a structural decomposition functional on the nonlinear space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 625 and the convergence rate is O(35/n^2).

Proof: Consider the structural decomposition operator L on the nonlinear space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 625 for all k. By the structural decomposition theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 35. QED.
